local DATA = table.concat(B)
local ALPHABET = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
local function decode64(s)
    local out = {}
    for i = 1, #s, 4 do
        local c1, c2 = s:sub(i, i), s:sub(i + 1, i + 1)
        local c3, c4 = s:sub(i + 2, i + 2), s:sub(i + 3, i + 3)
        local a = ALPHABET:find(c1, 1, true) - 1
        local b = ALPHABET:find(c2, 1, true) - 1
        local c = c3 == "=" and 0 or (ALPHABET:find(c3, 1, true) - 1)
        local d = c4 == "=" and 0 or (ALPHABET:find(c4, 1, true) - 1)
        local n = a * 262144 + b * 4096 + c * 64 + d
        out[#out + 1] = string.char(math.floor(n / 65536) % 256)
        if c3 ~= "=" then
            out[#out + 1] = string.char(math.floor(n / 256) % 256)
        end
        if c4 ~= "=" then
            out[#out + 1] = string.char(n % 256)
        end
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end
local source = decode64(DATA)
local chunk, compileError = loadstring(source)
if not chunk then
    error("[MountEiger V4] Compile failed: " .. tostring(compileError))
end
return chunk()
